# MODLOG

Running record of changes to the Star Wars: Republic Commando Rust and Bevy
rewrite. Newest entries first. Format and rules are in `MODLOG-template.md`.

## 2026-11-12: The objective tracker after loading a save, as the original (rc_scripts_plugin)

- **Changed:** `tracker::forget` (run when the level is left, which a load does) now calls the new `ObjectiveTracker::reset`, which puts everything back to a fresh helmet's values (the sight check's time and its last result too, which `hide` kept). Nothing is added to save files. docs/DESIGN.md "The objective tracker" says how the original handles saves; the line calling "not kept in saves" ours is gone.
- **Why:** The ask was to keep the tracker in saves the way the original does. The original's files show it doesn't keep it at all: the helmet that holds the tracker is in `var transient Helmet Helmet` (engine.u `PlayerController`), so it isn't saved, and after a load `PostLoadBeginPlay` calls `SpawnDefaultHUD`, which (ctgame.u `CTPlayer`) makes a `new class'Helmet'` from the class defaults. The Helmet defaults in engine.u set `bDraw`, `bBracketVisibleObjective` and `VisCheckFrequency` but not `bShowObjectiveTracker`, so it starts off. The tracker comes back at the next tracker step a script runs.
- **Where it comes from:** engine.u and ctgame.u script text and the Helmet class defaults and property flags (none of the tracker's Helmet properties are transient themselves; it is the reference to the helmet that is).
- **Tested how:** `cargo test -p rc_scripts_plugin` (new test `load_starts_from_a_fresh_helmet`).
- **Result:** All 41 `rc_scripts_plugin` tests pass.
- **Still broken / not tested:** Not tested by saving and loading in the game (the cloud build has no way to load a save from the command line); not tested on Windows.

## 2026-11-12: Dispensers drop in with dust, crush, are solid and ride their transports (rc_ai, rc_enemies, rc_model)

- **Changed:** New `rc_enemies/src/dispenser_world.rs`: `ride_bases` moves a dispenser with the mover its `AttachTag` names (new `rc_ai::Carry`, `Battle::set_dispenser_carry`, `dispenser_yaw`); `be_solid` adds the machines' cylinders to `LevelCollision` for bodies; `crush` deals whoever the machine touches their full health while it plays `entranceSky`, `EntranceSky704` or `EntranceSky384`. New `rc_model::NotifyEffect` and `Model::effects`: the intro animations' `AnimNotify_Effect`s start their effects (`Dispenser_Landing_Dirt`, `Dispenser_Landing_Metal`). `TransitionSound` and `DispenseSound` play when a map gives one. The drawn machine turns with its base. `rc_dispenser_view` puts a stand-in pawn where the machine lands and checks it is crushed and the landing effect starts. `rc_enemies` now uses `rc_movers` and `rc_movers_plugin`.
- **Why:** The coordinator asked to settle the open dispenser points from the game's files, decompiling where the files don't say (user's rule of 2026-11-11).
- **Where it comes from:** the script (`DroidDispenser.Dispense`, `ReleaseDroid`, `Bump`, `MakeTransition`, `GenerateActor`; `Pawn` state `Dispense`), the animations' notifies, the class defaults (TransitionInfo bytes decoded by hand), and Engine.dll read locally with a disassembler (`AttachToBone`, `DetachFromBone`, `UpdateAttachments`, `physRootMotion`, `UAnimNotify_Effect::Notify`, `UGameEngine::LoadMap`, `ULevel::MoveActor`). Details in docs/RESEARCH.md "Engine code read for the droid dispensers". Nothing decompiled is kept.
- **Tested how:** `cargo test -p rc_ai -p rc_enemies -p rc_model --lib --tests` (new `a_dispenser_rides_its_base_and_its_droids_ride_with_it`, `a_body_walking_into_the_machine_stops_at_its_side`); `rc_dispenser_check` (14 maps); `rc_dispenser_view yyy_35b 0` under xvfb; a scratch run (not committed) stepping ras_04b's two transport movers for 140 s with the carry.
- **Result:** All pass (rc_ai 45). `rc_dispenser_check`: 200 passed, 0 failed. `rc_dispenser_view yyy_35b 0`: 8 of 8 checks pass; `Dispenser_Landing_Dirt` starts at the machine's base 1.1 s into the drop with its `exp_large_Dirt` sound, and the stand-in is crushed (health 0). The scratch run: both ras_04b dispensers keep a fixed place on their transport the whole flight, the SBD one through its 45 degree turn. The droid's facing needed no change: the port already used the slot bone's X axis, which is what the engine does.
- **Still broken / not tested:** Not tested on Windows or in a played level; the transports were checked only in the scratch run, not in the game window. The falling machine isn't held up by who it lands on; sight lines pass through it; `Crushed` should kill the player outright (`bKillsNotIncapacitatesPlayer`) but the port treats it like any death. The warm-up's sound replaying every frame (offline `Tick`) isn't copied (no map has one). An effect notify's `Attach`, `OffsetRotation` and `DrawScale3D` aren't applied (none of the dispensers' effect notifies use them).
- **Next:** User check on Windows; droidekas as their own thread.

## 2026-11-12: Droid dispensers make droids (rc_ai, rc_enemies, rc_model)

- **Changed:** `rc_ai` reads `BDDispenser`, `SBDDispenser` and `DroidekaDispenser` actors as factories with their class defaults and map values (`Dispenser`, `Transition`), and runs their script in the battle (`rc_ai/src/dispense.rs`: intro, warm-up, online tag, waves, slots, release, unfold, damage steps, destruction, exhausted event, saves). The machine moves by its intro's root bone (`PHYS_RootMotion`). New `rc_enemies/src/dispensers.rs` draws it from `Dispensers.ukx`, swaps its skins, shows its effects, plays its animations and sounds, poses the droids riding out (`DispBreathe`, `DispUnfold`) and passes hits on it to the battle. New `rc_model::Model::notify_classes`, `posed_rooted`, `bone_from_origin_in_game`, `root_offset` (bones posed as the engine does: children of the `Move` bone don't follow it); droids riding out are placed even before they can be hit; `DroidLook::local_clip_rooted`, `root_travel`. New examples `rc_dispenser_check` and `rc_dispenser_view` (`--shots` picks picture moments). Save files keep each dispenser's state (`[enemies] spawners[].dispenser`).
- **Why:** Gap 4 of the gap list: the 29 dispensers in 14 maps made no droids.
- **Where it comes from:** the script text and class defaults in ctgame.u and engine.u, the model and its animations (release moments from the `AnimNotify_ReleaseDroid` notifies, slot paths from the `droidAttach` bones, the drop from the `Move` bone), the maps' values. Numbers in docs/RESEARCH.md "Droid dispensers". Nothing decompiled.
- **Tested how:** `cargo test -p rc_ai -p rc_enemies -p rc_model` (9 new dispenser tests); `rc_dispenser_check` on all 14 maps and `rc_dispenser_view yyy_35b 0` (window, pictures) in the cloud with the game's files copied read only from the PC.
- **Result:** All tests pass (rc_ai 44). `rc_dispenser_check`: 200 passed, 0 failed for 26 dispensers; 8 notes where the port's level collision has no floor under the machine (4 ride movers). `rc_dispenser_view`: the machine drops in, warms up, makes 3 droids that ride out and unfold, shows its damage effects and explodes with its destroyed skin; all 6 checks pass.
- **Still broken / not tested:** Not tested on Windows or in a played level. Movers, crushing, landing effects, sounds, the solid machine and the droid's facing are settled in the entry above. Not done: droidekas. The four skins share one picture; their differences are in shader parts the port doesn't draw.
- **Next:** User check on Windows; droidekas as their own thread.


## 2026-11-12: The two troopers on geo_01a's ramp die in the air strike, as the map says (rc_cast, rc_game, docs)

- **Changed:** No new game code was needed: since the cast can die (PR #131), the map's own scripts do the rest. New `rc_game --script` steps: send RunLZTroopers (as Trigger42 does when the player walks in), wait for CloneTrooper7 and 13 to reach WaitToBeBlownUp and WaitToBeBlownUp02, send ArialBombardment01, and check that both die. Comments and docs/DESIGN.md corrected: the level's script sequence carries on past its character's death (Mikey's own sequence kills him and then sends MikeyMegaForce; the ramp pair's sequences wait for their own death event and then play a line).
- **Why:** The user's side-by-side playtest: the two troopers on the ramp never died.
- **Where it comes from:** geo_01a.ctm (Trigger42, Trigger5, ScriptedSequence14 and 3, ProjectileFactory11 `GEO_01AProj` 400 damage / 512 uu), read with the level dump; the user's PC log shows the same run up to "0 characters reached" before #131.
- **Tested how:** `rc_game --script` in the cloud with the game's files.
- **Result:** "CloneTrooper7 0 uu from its place, alive; CloneTrooper13 0 uu from its place, alive -> PASS"; the missile at (-1455, 629, -2384) reaches them at 341 and 235 uu for 164.5 and 247.5 damage; both play Death and send GunshipTrooper02Death / 03Death (the earlier "can be hurt and die" check now accepts CloneTrooper7 being gone by then; 109 passed, the 20 other failures are the next-map, menu-focus, zap-hum and spoken-line checks that need what the cloud lacks), which run their sequences on to CT3R1001 / CT3R1002 (not loaded in the cloud: voice packages not copied); "CloneTrooper7 ... dead; CloneTrooper13 ... dead -> PASS".
- **Still broken / not tested:** Not tested on Windows. Their `SuggestEnemy Rockey` and `SetScriptPriority` are logged "not done yet" (the AI's part). No ragdoll: the original throws them (explosions always ragdoll); here they fall in place.
- **Next:** The troopers' own AI.
## 2026-11-12: The HUD blinks where the level scripts teach it (rc_scripts_plugin, rc_menus, rc_game, data/ui/hud.toml)

- **Changed:** The scripts' `BlinkHUDHealth`, `BlinkHUDSquad`, `BlinkHUDGrenade`, `BlinkHUDShields` and `BlinkHUDSquadCommandPrompt` steps are carried out. New `rc_scripts_plugin::hud_blink` (`LevelScript::hud_blink`) keeps which parts blink and for how long; new `blink` key for HUD elements in `data/ui/hud.toml` (health, squad, grenade, shields) and `rc_menus::hud::blink_alpha`; `rc_game`'s original HUD draws a blinking element with the blink's opacity. Marked in hud.toml: the four health-coloured meter parts (not their white backs), the squad panel, and the grenade panel, picture and count. New `rc_game --script` check "the script blinks the HUD's health meters".
- **Why:** geo_01a, geo_01b and geo_01c teach the HUD by blinking parts of it (7 steps); the port ignored them. Gap 6 in the missing-features audit.
- **Where it comes from:** the steps' script text in ctgame.u (find the player, call `BlinkHUDHealth(BlinkTime)` and so on; `BlinkTime` has no class default, so 0 when a map doesn't set it). What the blink does is compiled code, read from Engine.dll `UHelmet::DrawHUD` and `UHelmet::DrawTextInfo` (decompiled locally for reference, nothing committed): the time between drawings is added up and the blink stops once it is past `BlinkTime`; until then the part's opacity is `sin(5 t) * 200 + 55` (t the level's time), cut to a whole number and kept as a byte, so it wraps below 0. Which parts each blink touches is from the same function. The squad command prompt's flag is only cleared, so it shows nothing.
- **Tested how:** `cargo test -p rc_menus` (new `script_blinks_reach_the_original_parts` on the shipped layout, `the_opacity_wraps_like_a_byte`), `cargo test -p rc_scripts_plugin` (new `blinks_for_its_time_then_stops`); `rc_game --script` in the cloud with the game's files.
- **Result:** All tests pass. Game script: "the script blinks the HUD's health meters" passes (ScriptedTrigger3's step at script time 28.3 s; the four health-coloured meter parts drawn at opacity 211 while the white backs keep 130). The run's other failures (the level's spoken lines, the objective text, the menu walk-through) are in parts this change doesn't touch; the cloud ran at 1 to 4 frames a second.
- **Still broken / not tested:** Not tested on Windows or by eye. No shield element is marked `blink = "shields"` yet (no map blinks the shields, and the shields HUD is being changed in its own PR). The level's time is the scripts' clock, which starts with the level, as the original's does. The original's other pulses (health below half, the highlighted squad mate, the grenade count at 0) are not done.

## 2026-11-12: The objective tracker, the original's ring, arrow and distance (rc_scripts_plugin, rc_game, rc_game_core, rc_package, data/ui/objective_tracker.toml)

- **Changed:** New `rc_scripts_plugin::tracker`: the script step `ACTION_ShowActorObjectiveTracker` (before: "not done yet"), the lookup of each map's tracker targets, and the helmet's rules (sight check, ring or corner arrows, distance). New `rc_game/src/objective_tracker.rs` draws it with the original's pictures and font. New `rc_game_core::PlayerPawn` (the player body's centre and look, published by the player part), `ClassLibrary::object_path`, data file `data/ui/objective_tracker.toml`. `toml` moved to `rc_scripts_plugin`'s normal dependencies.
- **Why:** Gap 1 of the 2026-11-12 audit: 178 tracker steps in 36 maps did nothing, so the player was never shown where to go.
- **Where it comes from:** the step's UnrealScript text (ctgame.u), the Helmet defaults (engine.u), and the native drawing (Engine.dll `UHelmet::DrawHelmet`, `FCanvasUtil::DrawRotatedTile`/`DrawRotatedIcon`, `UHelmet::ActorDestroyed`), disassembled and partly emulated locally; checked against the user's screenshots of the original (tipped ring with "37.7", four inward triangles). Details in docs/DESIGN.md "The objective tracker". Nothing decompiled is in the repo.
- **Tested how:** `cargo test -p rc_scripts_plugin -p rc_game -p rc_package -p rc_game_core` (10 new tracker tests, including the ring corners against the values from running Engine.dll's code); target lookup over all 52 staged maps; `rc_game --map geo_01a` in the cloud with files copied read-only from the user's install, with screenshots.
- **Result:** All tests pass. In geo_01a at t=48 the log reads "ScriptedTrigger10 #1: objective tracker on BactaDispenserLZ (BactaDispenserGEO BactaDispenserGEO0 at (-988, -354, -2264))" then "objective tracker shows the ring (turn -16536, distance 38.0)"; the screenshot shows the tipped ring at the bottom middle, arrow pointing left (the dispenser is to the left), "38.0" in the middle. The corner arrows were checked by moving the goal in front of the view in a scratch build (not committed): four inward triangles round it, pulsing. 116 of 126 tracker tags across the maps find a placed actor; the other 10 have none in the original's map either.
- **Follow-up (same PR):** the sight line now passes blocking volumes that lines pass in the original (`bBlockZeroExtentTraces` off; new `MapCollision::line_through_triangles`, `rc_level_plugin::LineThroughVolumes`, test `sight_line_passes_blocking_volumes_but_not_walls`); a door or lift as the goal is followed as it moves. Read from Engine.dll and the scripts: nothing hides the tracker while the player is down (details in docs/DESIGN.md), so the port keeps showing it.
- **Still broken / not tested:** Not tested on Windows or by playing. Ours, not the original's: view yaw rounding (saves: see the entry above, which found the original does the same); the check runs in update, not while drawing.
- **Next:** User check on Windows: geo_01a, the bacta dispenser step.

## 2026-11-12: Enemies placed straight in the maps appear (rc_ai, rc_enemies, rc_cast, rc_game, data/enemies, data/cast)

- **Changed:** New `rc_ai::read_placed` and `Factory::placed`: every enemy placed straight in a map (no spawner) becomes a one-character spawner at its own place, with its own `Tag` (its class name when the map stores none), `Event`, `FirstDamagedEvent`, `StartState`, `TeamIndex` and `AIScriptTag`. `Battle` makes all of them on its first step (outside the one-per-step rule and the `max_alive_total` cap) and never again; events, `StopFactorySpawning`, `ResetFactory` and walk-in triggers leave them alone. They go after the real spawners so older saves still load. `rc_enemies` reads the game's classes once per run (`PawnClasses`) to tell which actors are characters, and `rc_enemies::placed_enemies` picks those an enemy file claims (placed clone troopers stay with the cast). New enemy file `geonosian_no_weapon.toml` for `GeonosianWarriorNoWeap` (no force pike, MaxHealth 300); `geonosian.toml` now claims only `GeonosianWarrior`. `cast.toml` leaves out `GrievousGuard*` so placed guards aren't drawn twice (new rc_cast test: every enemy class is left out of the cast). Scripts' `SetCollision` now reaches characters too (new `rc_game_core::CharacterCollision` message; `rc_enemies` takes the character out of shots and aiming while it is off), because geo_01a's script turns the intro Geonosian's collision off until its jump is done and the squad otherwise shot it at once. New example `rc_placed_enemies`; new `rc_game --script` check "the Geonosians placed in the map are made".
- **Why:** Item 23 of the gap list: 97 placed enemies on 24 maps never appeared, including geo_01a's Geonosian at the gunship door and Rocky, because only `PawnFactory` spawners were read.
- **Where it comes from:** Engine.u `Pawn.PostBeginPlay` (placed pawns get their controller at level start through their `AIScriptTag`'s script, unless `bDontPossess`; `Health = MaxHealth`; default inventory) and CTGame.u `PawnFactory.SetupNewActor` (which pawn fields a factory sets), read as text with `strings`; class defaults with the class library. Nothing copied into the repo.
- **Tested how:** `cargo test -p rc_ai -p rc_cast -p rc_enemies` (new: placed characters all there from the start, never made twice, untouched by events, older saves load; the unarmed Geonosian has its own file; every enemy class left out of the cast); `rc_placed_enemies` over all maps; `rc_game --script` in the cloud with the game's files.
- **Result:** All unit tests pass. `rc_placed_enemies`: 92 placed enemies made on 24 maps (the other 5 of the 97 are kinds without a file). `rc_game --script` in the cloud: 126 passed, 52 failed; main at the same base with the same staged files gives 125 passed and the same 52 failures (sound files not staged, and the menu/options checks after the level), so the only difference is the new check, which passes: Rocky is a `geonosian`, the intro warrior a `geonosian_no_weapon`, and its script turns its collision off, plays `WarriorIntroBreathe` and `WarriorIntro`, and turns it on again.
- **Still broken / not tested:** Not tested on Windows. `bDontPossess` characters are left out (none of the 92 set it). Placed kinds with no enemy file (droidekas, spider droids, Wookiees, vehicles) and placed clone troopers' AI are other threads. How the original's AI acts with no weapon at all is compiled code; our unarmed Geonosian just doesn't attack. The `max_alive_total` cap (12, a guess) counts placed enemies too, so on geo_05a (36 placed) spawners wait until some are dead.

## 2026-11-12: Placed clone troopers can be hurt and die (rc_cast, rc_game)

- **Changed:** Characters in the cast whose class has `Health` (read with the class's "...Defaults" class on top) get `rc_combat`'s `Health` and a `Hittable` cylinder from `CollisionRadius`/`CollisionHeight`, so bolts, blasts and the scripts' `HurtActor` hurt them. Vehicles are left unhurt for now. New `rc_cast::die`: on death the map's `Event` for the character is triggered (`RaiseEvent`), it stops, plays its `Death` animation and holds the last frame, and from then on ignores script orders apart from hiding and removal. The body is removed `RagdollLifeSpan` seconds later (60 for a clone trooper) unless `bSaveMyCorpse`. New `read::Life`, `Cast::is_dead`, `Cast::playing`, `Cast::death_event`. Two new `rc_game --script` checks.
- **Why:** The user's side-by-side playtest: the clone troopers never died. Before this the cast could not be hurt at all, so geo_01a's scripted deaths (Mikey, the trooper by the gunship) and the air strike did nothing.
- **Where it comes from:** engine.u's script text, read only (`Pawn.Died`, `PlayDying`, `PlayDyingAnim`, `TryToRagdoll`, `Controller.PawnDied`) and class defaults with `rc_probe --class` (CloneTrooper Health 100, CollisionHeight 84, CollisionRadius 40, RagdollLifeSpan 60). Notes in docs/RESEARCH.md "Clone troopers". Nothing copied.
- **Tested how:** `cargo test -p rc_cast`; `rc_game --script` in the cloud with the game's files, compared with the same run of PR #123.
- **Result:** New checks pass: "7 of 7 clone troopers have 100 health and can be hit; CloneTrooper7's death triggers GunshipTrooper02Death" and "CloneTrooper1 dead: true, marked Dead: true, playing Death". The level's own scripts now kill CloneTrooper15 (`DeadGunshipTrooper`, 500 damage at t=15.4) and Mikey (CloneTrooper5); both play Death and their bodies go 60 s later. 111 passed, 48 failed; the 48 are the same checks that fail in PR #123's run (the next maps aren't copied to the cloud, sound card, timing).
- **Still broken / not tested:** Not tested on Windows or by eye. Not done: ragdolls (the original always ragdolls a body killed by an explosion, so Mikey and the air-strike victims fly in the original but fall in place here), MegaForce (the push that throws Mikey), the electric and fire death animations, death sounds, death effects, dropping the rifle. Troopers still have no side (`Team`), so droids don't aim at them; that comes with their AI.
- **Next:** The two ramp troopers' script and the air strike that kills them; then the troopers' own AI.

## 2026-11-12: Clone troopers carry their own DC-15 rifle, not the commandos' DC-17m (rc_cast, data/enemies, data/weapons)

- **Changed:** New `data/weapons/dc15_blaster_clone.toml` (the game's `Properties.DC15BlasterClone`). `data/enemies/clone_trooper.toml` (class `CloneTrooper`) now uses it and holds its model (`held_weapon = "DC15BlasterClone"`, shot delay 0.15). `rc_cast` takes a character's `RequiredEquipment` from its class's "...Defaults" class in Properties/properties.u when that sets one, so the level's placed troopers hold the DC-15 too. `rc_enemy_look_check` now also loads each type's held weapon. The game script's rifle check looks for `DC15BlasterClone`.
- **Why:** User's side-by-side shots: the original's troopers hold their own blaster rifle, ours held the commando's DC-17m. `CloneTrooper`'s own value in ctcharacters.u is the DC-17m, but the game lays `CloneTrooperDefaults` over the class (core.dll holds `Properties.%sDefaults` and `UClass::IsDefaultsOverride`), and that sets `Properties.DC15BlasterClone`. The ammo boxes and player weapons already read their Defaults classes this way.
- **Tested how:** `rc_probe --class` on CloneTrooperDefaults, DC15BlasterClone, its ammo, DC15AttachmentClone, DC17BlasterProjDefaults (numbers in docs/RESEARCH.md "Clone troopers"); `strings` on core.dll for the Defaults rule; `cargo test --release -p rc_ai -p rc_cast -p rc_combat`; `rc_enemy_look_check`; `rc_game --script` in the cloud with the game's files copied read only from the PC.
- **Result:** rc_ai, rc_cast pass; rc_combat's `real_game_sounds_and_model` fails the same way on main without these changes (sound packages not copied to the cloud). Look check 47 passed, 0 failed, including "clone_trooper: holds DC15BlasterClone (model Weapons.CloneWeapons.DC15Blaster from DC15AttachmentClone)". Script: "the clone troopers hold their rifles: 8 of 8 clone troopers hold a DC15BlasterClone -> PASS".
- **Still broken / not tested:** Not tested on Windows or by eye. Only `RequiredEquipment` is taken from the Defaults class in the cast; its other values (footsteps, burnt skin, ragdoll settings) are not used yet. The troopers still don't die or fight with the game's own tactics (next PRs).
- **Next:** Placed troopers can be hurt and die.

## 2026-11-12: You stand in the gunship until the rope order, then drop to the ground (rc_level)

- **Changed:** `rc_level::volumes::blocking_volumes` also reads any other volume the map makes block players or characters (`bBlockPlayers` or `bBlockActors` set on the map's actor). These volumes don't stop shots.
- **Why:** The coordinator asked how the original gets you from the gunship to the ground. In the port you fell to the ground the moment the level started.
- **Where it comes from:** geo_01a's `PhysicsVolume1` (tag `PlayerStand`, `bBlockPlayers` true, a box under the player start, top at z -2096). The intro script's `SetCollision PlayerStand` ("This takes away the collision for the gunship animation") comes 2 s after `PlayPropAnim EntranceRope600`. engine.u's `Actor` defaults (both blocks off) and `BlockingVolume` (on). engine.u's `state Briefing` only turns the view, so the body still falls; `PhysicsVolume` gravity is -1100 and `Pawn.MaxFallSpeed` is 1200 (`TakeFallingDamage`). Nothing else in the scripts moves the player down.
- **Tested how:** `cargo test -p rc_level -p rc_level_plugin -p rc_music`. A scratch walk with `rc_movement` and geo_01a's collision (not committed): first with the volume, then after its collision goes, then with no volume from the start.
- **Result:** With the volume, you stand at z -2096. Once it goes, you leave it at once and land 1.07 s later at z -2688 at 1,141 units a second, with no damage. Without it (the old port), you were on the ground within 2 s of the start. geo_01a has one such volume; pro and geo_01briefing have none.
- **Still broken / not tested:** Not tested in the game window or on Windows. Only the maps copied to the cloud were checked for volumes like this.
- **Next:** User check on Windows.

## 2026-11-12: Level scripts give and take grenades (rc_combat, rc_scripts_plugin, rc_game_core, data/grenades)

- **Changed:** The scripts' `AddAmmo` step (the original's `ACTION_AddAmmo`) is carried out. `rc_scripts_plugin` reads `WhichWeapon` and `AmmoAmount` and sends `rc_game_core::pickups::AddAmmo`; new `rc_combat::script_ammo` finds the grenade whose file names that class (new `game_class` key in the four player grenade files) and changes the count with `Grenades::add_ammo`, kept between 0 and the most carried. Steps sent before the player has grenades wait until they do. New `rc_game --script` check "geo_01a starts with no grenades, as the map's script says".
- **Why:** geo_01a's opening script takes every grenade away (ScriptedTrigger52: FlashBang -5, EMPGrenade -2, SonicDetonator -2, ThermalDetonator -2), and later levels hand out 5 of each; the port ignored the step, so the player started with 2 of each. Gap 6 in the missing-features audit.
- **Where it comes from:** `ACTION_AddAmmo` in gameplay.u (find the weapon with `FindInventoryType`; if not carried, nothing happens; else `AmmoType.AddAmmo`) and `Ammunition.AddAmmo` in engine.u (`Min(MaxAmmo, AmmoAmount + n)`, then not below 0), read from the game's script text. Each grenade's class and ammo name read with `rc_probe --class`. All 40 uses across the maps name one of the four grenades.
- **Tested how:** `cargo test -p rc_combat --lib` (new `geo_01a_starts_with_no_grenades`, `steps_wait_for_the_player`, `script_add_ammo_stays_between_none_and_the_most`), `cargo test -p rc_scripts_plugin`; `rc_game --script` in the cloud with the game's files copied read only from the PC.
- **Result:** All tests pass. Game script: the new check passes, "script steps [FlashBang -5, EMPGrenade -2, SonicDetonator -2, ThermalDetonator -2]; carried thermal_detonator 0, sonic_detonator 0, emp_grenade 0, flash_grenade 0".
- **Still broken / not tested:** Not tested on Windows. A step naming a weapon (not a grenade) is logged and skipped; no map uses one. The port still gives each level fresh grenades, where the original carries them from level to level, so the later levels' "+5" and "-n" start from 2. Loading the level-start save (made before the scripts run) has not been tested with this.
- **Next:** The scripts' BlinkHUD teaching steps (separate PR).

## 2026-11-12: The script's "play prop animation" no longer holds the opening back (rc_scripts, rc_scripts_plugin)

- **Changed:** `PlayPropAnim` no longer makes the level script wait (it waited our fixed 1.5 s each time). It now plays `BaseAnim` once on every cast member whose tag is `AnimPropTag`. On the player's own body (`PlayerCommando`) it is logged and nothing is drawn. New `ScriptStats::player_body_anims`.
- **Why:** The user said the opening on the gunship isn't like the original's. The gap audit found the rope animation `EntranceRope600` (and three `ActionBreathe`s) logged as "not done yet".
- **Where it comes from:** gameplay.u's `ACTION_PlayPropAnim` source text (`extends ScriptedAction`, `ForEach AllActors(class'Actor', A, AnimPropTag) A.PlayAnim(...)`, `return false`). ctcharacters.u says the player's tag is `PlayerCommando`. engine.u: `Pawn` sets `bOwnerNoSee`, and the first-person camera is `Location + EyePosition()` (`EyeHeight`, bob, shake), with no bone. No class from `PlayerCommando` up to `Pawn` changes either. No actor in geo_01a has the tags `CloneRope62` or `CloneRope40`.
- **Tested how:** `cargo test -p rc_scripts -p rc_scripts_plugin` (new `play_prop_anim_does_not_hold_the_script`); `rc_script_check geo_01a` before and after, with the game's files in the cloud.
- **Result:** Tests pass. geo_01a's opening events now come 4.6 to 6 s sooner, at the original's pace: `SpawnLandingZone` at 0.05 s (was 4.65 s), `Rappel` at 3.10 s (was 7.65 s), `GunshipIdle` at 9.10 s (was 15.15 s).
- **Still broken / not tested:** Not tested in the game window or on Windows. The rope animation itself shows nothing, as in the original's first-person view. How the player gets from the gunship to the ground (the script takes away `PlayerStand`'s collision) wasn't checked here. `PlayPropAnim` on level props drawn by `rc_level_plugin::anim_props` isn't routed yet; no geo_01a prop needs it.
- **Next:** User check on Windows.

## 2026-11-12: geo_01a's gunship rests where the original's does, and the ground dust (rc_anim, rc_model, rc_cast, rc_effects, rc_effects_plugin, rc_scripts_plugin)

- **Changed:** Characters and vehicles the cast draws no longer show the travel of their skeleton's `Move` bone, unless their `Physics` is 12 (root motion): `rc_anim::globals_in_game`, `rc_anim::move_bone`, `rc_model::Model::posed_in_game` and `bone_frame_in_game`, `rc_cast::read::CastMember::root_motion`. New `rc_scripts_plugin/src/placed_emitters.rs`: every effect a map places as an actor (`Emitter` classes) runs from the start of the level at its map place and turn; a level event naming its tag triggers it as engine.dll does (each emitter with `TriggerDisabled` flips `Disabled`; a disabled emitter is neither moved nor drawn; `ResetOnTrigger` starts it over). New `rc_effects::read::Library::is_emitter`, `SpawnEffect::endless`, `rc_effects_plugin::TriggerEffect`, `EffectState::trigger`, `EmitterDef::trigger_disabled` and `reset_on_trigger`.
- **Why:** User side by side: the landing gunship in geo_01a sat too high, and the dust under it was missing.
- **Where it comes from:** geo_01a's map (GunshipHigh1's place, its `GunshipAI` ScriptedSequence, `REP_Gunship_GroundDust0`'s place and turn); ctcharacters.u's `Gunship` (`Physics` 4, flying); the gunship clips in vehicles.ukx (Move bone 757 to 850 units up); engine.dll decompiled for reference only, in a scratch folder (`USkeletalMesh::PostLoad`, `USkeletalMeshInstance::ApplyAnimation`, `AActor::physRootMotion`): children of the Move bone are placed without its travel unless the actor uses root motion. The trigger rule from `UParticleEmitter::execTrigger`, `UParticleEmitter::Reset`, `AEmitter::Tick` and `AEmitter::Render`, with engine.u's `ParticleEmitter` property order giving the bits' names. Nothing decompiled is in the repository.
- **When it flies off (from the files):** It doesn't leave during the opening. Its script plays `WarriorIntroGunshipBreathe` from the start, `WarriorIntroGunship` and then the hovering `WarriorIntroGunshipLoop` when the intro script sends `GunshipIdle` (after the briefing and the rope), and only plays `GunshipAmbushSpiderdroid` when `GunshipAttack` comes, after the bunker door is destroyed and the player reaches the spider droid area. Our port already plays this script; no change needed.
- **Tested how:** `cargo test -p rc_anim -p rc_model -p rc_cast -p rc_scripts_plugin -p rc_effects -p rc_effects_plugin` (new tests: the gunship dust read as the map places it; a trigger switching an emitter off and on); a scratch probe posing the gunship clips both ways (not committed).
- **Result:** All tests pass. Probe: the ship is now drawn 757 to 850 units lower than before (the Move bone's height in each frame), at the map's place; the player start is on its door step. geo_01a: 11 placed emitters read.
- **Still broken / not tested:** Not tested in the game window, on Windows or in the cloud (the cloud copy has no textures). Not done: the map's own copies of emitter settings, the emitter's `DrawScale`, saving which emitters are off. By the same engine rule, the end-of-level fly-off clip now only turns the ship in place (nothing in the files switches it to root motion). Placed emitters now run on every map, not just geo_01a.
- **Next:** User check on Windows.

- **Changed:** New `rc_effects_plugin::held` (`HeldModel`: read a weapon class's `AttachmentClass` model, place it on bone `weaponAttach_R`, spawn it), moved out of `rc_cast`, which now uses it. New `[look] held_weapon` in enemy files (the original's `RequiredEquipment`): `DC17mBlaster` for `clone_trooper.toml`, `DC15BlasterCloneTM` for `clone_trooper_tm.toml`, empty for every other type. `rc_enemies` gives each new fighter of such a type the model as a child (`HeldWeapon`) and moves it onto the weapon bone every time the body is posed (`DroidLook::bone_frame`). ## 2026-11-11: Clone troopers fight the droids (rc_ai, rc_enemies, data/enemies, data/weapons)

- **Changed:** Enemy types have a side (`[type] side`, "separatist" or "republic", the original's TeamIndex). New files `data/enemies/clone_trooper.toml` (`CloneTrooper`) and `clone_trooper_tm.toml` (`CloneTrooperTM`), and their rifle `data/weapons/dc15_blaster_clone_tm.toml`, every number read from the game's class defaults. `rc_ai::Battle` runs both sides: droids fight the player, the squad and the troopers; troopers fight the droids only. Each side keeps its own line of fire clear (`ally_in_line` now widens with the shooter's aim error), and has its own cap on how many are alive. Troopers get `Team::Republic`, so the crosshair and the squad treat them as friends; they don't count for the battle music. New `[combat] can_crouch` (the original's `bCanCrouch`, off for troopers). A loaded save puts fighters that were attacking straight back into attacking. `rc_droidview`'s stand-in no longer shoots troopers. The game script's save round-trip check no longer compares what a running fight changes every frame (facing, mode, last sighting, a fighting one's position).
- **Why:** geo_01a's 16 clone trooper spawners made nothing, so the trench battle had no troopers fighting.
- **Tested how:** `rc_probe --class` on CloneTrooper, CloneTrooperTM, CloneTrooperBot, CTBot, StateTrandoshanAttack, DC15BlasterCloneTM and its ammo and bolt (numbers in docs/RESEARCH.md "Clone troopers"); `cargo test -p rc_ai` (new `clone_troopers_and_droids_fight_each_other_not_the_player`); `rc_enemy_look_check`; `rc_droidview geo_01a --seconds 120 --check` and `rc_game --script` in the cloud with the game's files.
- **Result:** rc_ai 35 passed. Look check: both trooper files load the CloneTrooper model, its own skin and 11 of 11 animations. rc_droidview: every CHECK passed; 9 troopers against 15 droids, 98 trooper shots of which 41 hit droids and 0 hit other troopers, 11 droid bolts hit troopers. `rc_game --script` (cloud): 136 passed, 4 failed, the same 4 as main at 0706863 with the same staged files (the level's spoken lines reaching the sound card, and the objective text; both need things the cloud lacks).
- **Still broken / not tested:** Not tested on Windows or by eye. Not in the files: the troopers' attack state (`StateTrandoshanAttack`) works in compiled code, so they fight the way our droids do; whether a fallen trooper drops its DC-17m. The troopers hold no rifle (the cast's held-weapon code isn't used for spawned fighters yet) and have no voices (the original's classes set none). The placed (cast) troopers still don't fight.
- **Next:** Held rifles for spawned fighters; user check on Windows.

## 2026-11-11: The bacta dispenser's zap and hum (rc_scripts_plugin, rc_effects, rc_effects_plugin, rc_model, rc_level_plugin)

- **Changed:** Health now goes up only once the dispenser's `Load` animation (the canister swinging in) has finished, as the game's script does (`bActivated` is set after `FinishAnim`). Before, healing started at once and was nearly over when the zap appeared. While you heal, the dispenser shows the game's own zap effect (`BactaZap_Dispenser`: mist, beams, glow, the little hologram clone, power bars, the BACTA text, lightning) hung on its `Eye` bone, and its hum (`ele_GEO_genEnrgy_lp_01`, the effect's `AmbientSound`) loops. Both start when the `Load` animation has finished and vanish at once when you stop. New: `rc_effects::def::EffectDef::ambient_sound`; `SpawnEffect::axes` and `vanish_with_carrier`; ambient sounds loop in the effects plugin; `rc_model::Model::bone_from_origin`; `AnimProp::bone` and `pose_now`; `bacta::zap`, `BactaZap`, `BactaStats::zaps`.
- **Why:** Follow-up to the bacta dispenser work (PR #96); the user asked for it.
- **Where it comes from:** the timing and the bone are the game's `BactaDispenser` script text in ctgame.u (`state Active`: `FinishAnim(); SpawnAttachment('Eye', DispenserEffectClass)`; `EndState`: `DestroyAttachment('Eye')`). The effect class is each dispenser class's `DispenserEffectClass` default in `Properties/MarkerProperties.u` (all five set `BactaZap_Dispenser`). The hum is `BactaZap_Dispenser`'s `AmbientSound` default in `Properties/CTEffects.u`. All read with `rc_probe --classes` and `rc_effect_check`; nothing copied into the repo.
- **Tested how:** `cargo test` for the changed crates; `rc_game --script` in the cloud with the game's files copied read only from the PC (new check "the game's zap effect hung on the dispenser's Eye bone while healing, with its hum, and was taken away after").
- **Result:** All the bacta checks pass, including the new one: `BactaZap_Dispenser` hung on BactaDispenserGEO0's Eye bone at (-1025, -321, -2241), its hum `ele_GEO_genEnrgy_lp_01` started, and both were gone when health was full. Picture from a slowed-down run: the blue beam stands in the dispenser while healing (`/mnt/project-files/bacta-dispenser/zap_while_healing.png`, not in the repo). Other checks that fail in this cloud run fail on main too: maps not copied (geo_01b, pro), and timed checks (the game runs slowly in the cloud).
- **Still broken / not tested:** Not seen or heard on Windows. The player's body effect (`BactaZap_Dispenser_Body`) is not shown: its particles start at the bones of the player's own body model, which first person here doesn't have. The hum's loudness is the effects plugin's distance rule (our choice), not 3D. Found while reading: the game heals at `MaxHealth / BactaRate` per second (BactaRate 4, so a full heal takes 4 seconds); our `heal_per_second = 50` is a separate setting and was not changed here.

It opens the original's `MenuSelectProfile` page; `< NEW >` opens `MenuPCNewProfileName` (type a name), creating or choosing a profile shows `MenuLoadingProfile` for its one second, and the active-profile and name-in-use messages use `MenuWarning`. With no profile chosen the game opens on PROFILES and BACK is hidden, as `CTMenuMain.Init` does. Guesses: Enter in the name box moves to OK (the original picks the "nearest" widget; which of BACK and OK it lands on wasn't worked out); the warning and loading boxes borrow the page's background as the question box does; the original's fade before the loading box isn't drawn; name match ignores capitals. Profiles hold only saves: settings and unlocked levels stay shared (the original keeps them per profile in `User.ini`). New `rc_combat::hands` and `data/combat/hand_signal.toml` (one section per use, so door panels etc. can be added as data), `UseItem::hands` (set by the bacta script), `ArmsInput::hold_fire`, `ArmsHistory` (every arms animation started, for tests and mods). Finding: in the shipped hudarms.ukx the three bacta moves are 3-frame (0.1 s) poses with every point of the hands below and behind the eye, out of view (the keypad and squad-order moves in the same set do come into view). So on screen the gun goes down, no hands show, and the gun comes back. No visible arm-plugging animation exists in the game's files.

- **Result:** rc_menus tests pass (47) and rc_game program tests pass (67); clippy shows nothing new. The numbers match the original's formula: 0 at the start, 44 at 0.1 s, 110 at 0.25 s, 220 from 0.5 s.

## 2026-11-11: The menu background video no longer slows the game to a crawl (rc_video build settings, rc_game)

- **Changed:** The video decoder (`rc_video`) and the oxideav crates it uses are built optimised in debug builds too (root `Cargo.toml`, `[profile.dev.package.*]`). `menu_video::play` decodes at most 3 frames per game frame (`MAX_DECODES_PER_UPDATE`); when it is further behind, the video runs late instead (logged once, counted in `MenuVideo::frames_late`, shown in the script's video check).
- **Why:** The cloud's `rc_game --script` run fell from about 5 frames a second to under 1 after the menu video (#102) merged. A debug build decoded one 512x512 frame in about 70 ms, so it could never keep up with 30 a second, and `play` decoded every missed frame to catch up, so each game frame took longer than the last (gdb showed the time inside `rc_video::msmpeg4v2` called from `menu_video::play`, catching up to frame 401).
- **Tested how:** `rc_video_check menu_bg.avi` before and after; `cargo test -p rc_game --bins menu_video` (new `a_slow_computer_lets_the_video_run_late_instead_of_catching_up`); `rc_game --script` in the cloud with the game's files.
- **Result:** Debug decode 69.6 ms a frame before, 7.6 ms after; release 4.8 ms (unchanged). Main menu in the cloud script: under 1 frame a second (frames up to 55 s long) before, 6 to 11 frames a second after.
- **Still broken / not tested:** Not tested on Windows. The user's PC runs a release build, which already decoded fast enough, so it should see no change. Inside geo_01a the cloud run is still slow (about 1 frame a second); that is the software renderer drawing the new default graphics settings and other new effects, looked at separately.

## 2026-11-11: Fix the crash on geo_01a's load with BLUR EFFECTS on (rc_level_viewer)

- **Changed:** `FalloffMaterial` (the light shafts and god rays from the game's falloff shader) no longer takes part in Bevy's depth/motion prepass or in shadows (`enable_prepass` and `enable_shadows` return false, as `rc_visor` and `rc_shadows` already do).
- **Why:** With BLUR EFFECTS on (the default since the GRAPHICS page change), motion blur turns on a prepass. Bevy's prepass pipeline for this material took the vertex layout set in `specialize`, which has no slot for the colours the prepass shader reads, so the graphics card refused it and the game quit as geo_01a loaded: `graphics error (Validation) ... 'prepass_pipeline' ... Location[7] Float32x4 ... not provided by the previous stage`.
- **Tested how:** `rc_game --script` in the cloud with the game's files, before (main at 5edb977) and after.
- **Result:** Before: the run quit at 32.9 s with the error above, as geo_01a finished loading. After: geo_01a loaded and the script played on in the level for 2.5 minutes with no graphics error (the run was then stopped; its slowness is a separate fix).
- **Still broken / not tested:** Not tested on Windows. The shafts are added light, so leaving them out of the prepass and shadows changes nothing on screen (motion blur doesn't smear them; it didn't before either, since the game quit).

- **Tested how:** `rc_game --script` in the cloud with geo_01a; the bacta dispenser picture (two troopers) before and after.

- **Still broken / not tested:** Not tested on Windows or by playing. Troopers hold no rifles yet (no character in the port draws a held weapon yet); that and their fighting the droids are next. Not compared with the original from the same spot.

## 2026-11-11: The visor's frame and compass, drawn on the helmet's glass (rc_game, rc_menus, data/ui/hud.toml)

- **Changed:** The arcs down both sides of the visor, the short pieces at the top and the sliding compass strip, at the places, sizes, pictures and colours of the original's Engine.dll (`UHelmet::DrawHUD`). The HUD's glass parts are now drawn onto a 4:3 page (`helmet::HudPage`, its own camera) laid on the helmet model's glass, as the original does, instead of being placed point by point. New HUD keys: `scroll` (a repeating, sliding picture) and group `visor` (may overlap).

The low-health pulse, the top-left ring and the shield bars are not done. The cloud game ran at about 1 frame a second on main as well (before this change), so timed checks in the cloud fail for that reason.

## 2026-11-11: The rifle starts full, 300 shots, as in the original (rc_combat, data/weapons/dc17m_blaster.toml)

- **Changed:** Weapons the player is given at the level's start now carry the most they can (`MaxAmmo`: 300 for the DC-17m) instead of a lying pickup's `PickupAmmoCount` (120). New `WeaponState::given`, used by `equip_player`. Weapons picked up in the level still hold `PickupAmmoCount`.
- **Why:** User's screenshots: the original's gun counter reads 300 at geo_01a's start, ours 120.
- **Tested how:** Read from the original's engine.u script text (`Weapon.GiveAmmo`: in single player a non-secondary weapon gets `InitAmmoAmount`, and when that is -1, as for every weapon here, `MaxAmmo`; only a secondary weapon gets `PickupAmmoCount`) and `rc_probe --class` for the numbers; `cargo test -p rc_combat --test range`.

- **Still broken / not tested:** Not tested on Windows or in a full level run.

## 2026-11-11: Light shafts and god rays with the game's falloff shader (rc_level_viewer, rc_level_plugin)

- **Changed:** New `rc_level_viewer::falloff` (+ `falloff.wesl`): `HsFalloff` materials get the game's own shader maths: picture x `DiffuseTint` x falloff gradient (by viewing angle) x `GradientTint` x the mesh's painted colours, squared when `SrcBlend` is `SRCCOLOR`, panned and oscillated by the material's rates, added on top, no baked light and no fog. Settings and defaults read at load from Engine.u; `FalloffPlugin` comes with `PropLightPlugin`. Logs `light shafts ... N prop parts`.
- **Why:** User's side-by-side screenshot of geo_01a: "the god rays aren't implemented". The sun rays (`SunBeam`, `rayfalloff`) were drawn as a flat block under the zone fog, or not seen.
- **Tested how:** `falloff_check` with the game's files (cloud); `cargo test -p rc_level_viewer --lib falloff` (3 tests); `rc_level_viewer geo_01a --capture` before and after from the same spot (cloud).
- **Result:** falloff_check 11 passed, 0 failed (9 prop parts on geo_01a). The capture shows streaky shafts fading along the beam, over the haze, where there was a flat light block (fog on) or nothing visible.
- **Still broken / not tested:** Not tested on Windows. Our choices: the mesh's painted colours as the vertex colour; no fog on shafts; the shaft colour added as it is (the game adds on its brightness curve). The prologue's tank liquid also uses `HsFalloff`; not looked at.
- **Next:** User check against the original on geo_01a.

## 2026-11-11: The landing gunship hovers from its map place (rc_cast, rc_game)

- **Changed:** The cast part reads each class's `bAlignBottom`. Where it is off (the `Gunship` class, so geo_01a's `GunshipHigh1`), the model is drawn from its origin at the map's place, as a prop is, instead of being stood with its lowest point `CollisionHeight` below. New script check "the landing gunship hovers where the map puts it" and picture `logs/gunship.png` (the player stood 1500 uu away with a clear view, looking at it).
- **Why:** User's side-by-side screenshots of geo_01a: in the original the landing gunship hovers over the landing zone. The gunship was already drawn and already played its level script animations (`ScriptedSequence4`: `WarriorIntroGunshipBreathe`, `WarriorIntroGunship`, `WarriorIntroGunshipLoop`); the one difference found in the files is `bAlignBottom = false` on `Gunship` (engine.u's comment: "Actor aligned to bottom of cylinder").
- **Tested how:** `rc_probe --class GunshipHigh` and `--class Pawn` with the install's files; `rc_game --script` in the cloud with geo_01a.
- **Result:** 137 passed, 0 failed. `GunshipHigh1 drawn from (2020, 1585, -1932), map place (2020, 1585, -1932)` (before: about 100 uu lower). The picture shows it hovering high over the landing zone, playing its loop.
- **Still broken / not tested:** Not tested on Windows or by playing. Not compared with the original from the same spot (the user's screenshot's spot isn't known). The trooper on the rope under it hangs sideways; that is the clone troopers' job (next PR). The gunship's later `GunshipAmbushSpiderdroid` animations (event `GunshipAttack`) weren't reached in the self-test.

## 2026-11-11: Squad, grenade and health meters as the original draws them (rc_menus, rc_game, rc_game_core, rc_squad_plugin, data/ui/hud.toml)

- **Changed:** The bottom-left squad panels, the two health meters and the bottom-right grenade panel now use the exact places, sizes, pictures and colours from the original's Engine.dll (`UHelmet::DrawHUD`, helmet on), drawn through the helmet's glass. Squad panels show only for mates actually in the squad (new `SquadMember::in_squad`). Meter and squad colours follow the original's four health levels (`APawn::CalcHealthLevel`: below half, below 0.91, above); the meter bar drops in elevenths. Grenade pictures by class fixed (thermal, sonic, EC, flash were mixed up); the count turns red at zero. The ammo numbers under the crosshair's right are gone: the original shows them only on the gun. The guessed shield tick column is gone (the original's shield bars come next). New HUD keys `colour_by`, `colours`, `fill_steps`, `fill_least_gap`; texts `{health_level}`, `{squad_health_level}`; condition `grenades_left`. `GlassMap::place` now carries points past the glass edge on with the nearest glass piece.
- **Why:** User's side-by-side screenshots: squad icons, "58 60" and "2" showed when the original's didn't; meters looked dim and different.
- **Tested how:** Numbers read from Engine.dll in a scratch folder outside the repo (nothing of it committed); `cargo test -p rc_menus -p rc_game -p rc_game_core`; `rc_probe --hud` compared with the user's screenshot of the original (meters and grenade panel land within a few pixels); `rc_game --script` in the cloud.
- **Result:** Tests pass; "the original HUD is drawn in its own colours" passes (3771 of 9138 meter pixels in a health colour).
- **Still broken / not tested:** Not tested on Windows. Pulsing, the arrow pointing to each squad mate, the timer ring, and which order uses which squad picture are not done. In our geo_01a two squad mates are already in the squad at the start, so their panels show; in the original you start alone (a squad question, not the HUD).

## 2026-11-11: The original's crosshair pictures and colours (rc_game, rc_combat, rc_game_core, data/ui/hud.toml)

- **Changed:** Each weapon now uses its class's own `Reticle` from ctinventory.u (DC-17m: `HUD_WeaponDefault`, the ring without the dot; we used the "PC" pictures, which no weapon names). Drawn at 0.8 of our old size (the picture's size on the original's 600-line HUD). Tinted as the original's helmet does (Engine.dll `UHelmet::DrawHelmet`, by target type): (200,240,255) at opacity 110 normally, (130,255,130) at 220 over a squad mate, (255,130,130) at 220 over an enemy. New `WeaponReadout::on_friend`; `[crosshair]` keys `colour`, `friend_colour`, `enemy_colour`.

## 2026-11-11: Advisor's words and the objective line as in the original (rc_menus, rc_game, rc_scripts_plugin, data/ui/hud.toml)

- **Changed:** The advisor's words now show near the top left in the game's own font (`OrbitBold15` at 0.8 size), yellow (200,200,64), broken onto lines to fit a 364-wide box, fading in over 0.5 s; the bottom-middle box with Bevy's font is now only the placeholder. The current mission objective ("USE BACTA TO HEAL") shows centred in the same box at full size in (164,200,208) when nobody speaks. While the advisor speaks, the short messages and use prompt in that box are hidden, as in the original. Both are moved through the helmet's glass as the original draws its HUD (lower and slightly curved). New: `helmet::GlassMap`, `rc_scripts_plugin::objectives` (the map's `MissionObjectives` list, revealed by its tag event, completed/failed/changed by `ACTION_ObjectiveUpdate`, saved with the game), HUD element keys `font`, `wrap`, `glass`, texts `{subtitle}` `{objective}`, `[layout] text_fade_in`.
- **Why:** User's side-by-side screenshots: the advisor text was a bottom-middle monospace line and the objective line was missing.
- **Tested how:** Positions, sizes, colours and the fade read from the original's Engine.dll (`UHelmet::DrawTextInfo`, `DrawSubtitleText`, `DrawCurrentObjectiveText`) and engine.u/gameplay.u scripts in a scratch folder outside the repo (nothing of it committed); `cargo test -p rc_menus -p rc_scripts_plugin`; `rc_game --script` in the cloud.
- **Result:** New check "the current objective is drawn at the top, as in the original" passes in the cloud (17 letters, top 14% down, centred). `rc_probe --hud` paints the advisor's words with the same line breaks as the user's screenshot of the original. Other script failures in this cloud run come from game files not copied to the cloud (sounds, menus).

## 2026-11-11: Each zone's distance fog, from the map (rc_level_viewer, rc_level_plugin, rc_game_core, rc_visor)

- **Changed:** New `rc_level_viewer::fog`: every `ZoneInfo`/`LevelInfo`'s `bDistanceFog`, `DistanceFogColor`, `DistanceFogStart`/`End`, `DistanceFogBlendTime`, with missing values from `ZoneInfo`'s defaults in Engine.u (read at load). The player's camera (and the viewer's; `--no-fog` to compare) shows the fog of the zone it is in, as Bevy linear fog. The visor's low light fog now marks the camera `rc_game_core::OwnFog`, and zone fog leaves it alone meanwhile. Log: `level: distance fog on the player's camera: ...`.
- **Why:** The original's geo_01a has a brown haze over the far arena that the port lacked. The arena zone (`ZoneInfo12`) stores fog on, colour 142,119,94, end 64000 (start default 3000). The game's D3DDrv.dll sets Direct3D linear fog, the same formula Bevy uses.
- **Tested how:** `cargo test -p rc_level_viewer fog` (2 tests); `rc_level_viewer geo_01a --capture` (cloud); `rc_game --script` in the cloud.
- **Result:** Tests pass. geo_01a: 3 of 5 zones fog, camera starts in the arena zone with 142,119,94 from 3000 to 64000; the far tiers fade into brown as in the original. geo_01b: 11 of 13 zones, 136,135,119 from 100 to 10000. Script: 132 passed, 2 failed (the same 2 level-line checks); the visor check (no low light fog after leaving low light) passes.
- **Still broken / not tested:** Not tested on Windows. Our choices, not read from the game: the camera's zone picks the fog; zone changes blend in a straight line over `DistanceFogBlendTime`; no fog in the sky box; `bClearToFogColor` unused; fog mixed in linear light by distance (Direct3D: on screen colours, by depth). geo_01b's near fog (from 100 units) not compared with the original.
- **Next:** User check on Windows against their screenshots; the original's bloom (zones also set `Bloom`/`BloomFilter`).

## 2026-11-11: Geonosis arena tiers drawn as stone, not grey-white (rc_level_viewer)

- **Changed:** Materials that are a `Combiner` with `CombineOperation` 2 (`CO_Multiply` in Engine.u's `EColorOperation`) now multiply their two pictures, each on its own texture coordinate set (`materials::Layer`, `Batch::layer_uvs`, `PropMaterial` binding 101/102). `TexCoordSource` (`TCS_Stream0`-`7`) now picks the coordinate set; `CO_Use_Color_From_Material2` shows `Material2`. New check `examples/multiply_check.rs`; the viewer logs `multiplying materials: N prop parts`.
- **Why:** User's side-by-side screenshots of geo_01a: the arena's far tiers, spires and rocks were flat grey-white in the port, brown stone in the original. Those props (`EntireArena`, `RezSpires`, `UpperRock`, `upperStair`, bUnlit) use materials like `Arena_Textures.Unique.TierWallLM` = painted light picture (set 0) x stone (`TierWall`, set 1); we drew only the light picture.
- **Tested how:** `multiply_check` (cloud); `rc_level_viewer geo_01a --capture` before and after (cloud, the game's files); `prop_light_check`; `rc_game --script` in the cloud.
- **Result:** multiply_check 4 passed, 0 failed (plain 200, multiplied 48 against 50 expected, on both halves). geo_01a: 28 prop parts, 14 batches multiplied; the tiers show stone, dark brown as in the original. prop_light_check 9 passed. Script: 132 passed, 2 failed (the 2 level-line checks that already failed).
- **Still broken / not tested:** Not tested on Windows. Combiners that blend two pictures by vertex alpha (`CO_AlphaBlend_With_Mask`, rock and plaster on `RezSpires`, `RubbleBlend`) still show the first picture only. Combiners on walls (BSP) and terrain not done.
- **Next:** The arena zone's distance fog (next entry).

## 2026-11-11: Projectile spawners: air bombardment and bunker missiles in geo_01a (rc_effects, rc_effects_plugin, rc_scripts_plugin, rc_game)

- **Changed:** The maps' `ProjectileFactory` actors now run with the same spawner rules as effect spawners (`rc_effects::factory` gains `Kind::Projectile`, spawn-point `InitialPhysics`/`InitialSpeed`, the factory's `InitialPhysicsFromFactory`/`InitialSpeedFromFactory`, and `ACTION_StopFactorySpawning` with `PermanentlyDisableFactory` and `DestroyFactoryOffspring`). New `rc_effects::projectile` reads projectile classes and their parents from properties.u, physicalmaterials.u, ctinventory.u and engine.u and moves them (straight, or falling with gravity -1100 up to 12000). New `rc_scripts_plugin/src/projectiles.rs` draws the class's model, carries its `ProjectileEffect` trail, sweeps against the level and on a blast spawns the class's hit effect and sends `rc_combat::Explode` with its `Damage`/`DamageRadius`. `SpawnEffect` gains `follow` (the effect moves with an entity and fades out when it goes, as `Emitter.FadeOut`). Two new script checks. The save round trip now skips `effect_spawners` (geo_01a's looping `ScriptedTrigger13` switches `FXFactory3` on and off between the two saves, which failed about half the cloud runs since #100). The crosshair check also counts the reticle's pale blue tint from #101, which over dark rock never reached its "white" level.
- **Why:** User's side-by-side screenshots of geo_01a: no laser combat or explosions in the arena.
- **Tested how:** Rules read from ctgame.u (`Factory`, `ProjectileFactory`, `ACTION_StopFactorySpawning`), engine.u (`Projectile`, `Emitter`, `PhysicsVolume`) and ctinventory.u (`CTProjExplosive`) in a scratch folder (nothing committed). `cargo test -p rc_effects -p rc_effects_plugin -p rc_scripts_plugin` (with the game files for the class reader); `rc_game --script` in the cloud with geo_01a.
- **Result:** Script: 135 passed, 0 failed; after bringing in main, 136 passed, 0 failed. Bunker missiles (`GEO_01A_BunkerMissile`): 2 made, 2 blew up on the level within 0.7 s, with trail and `Explosion_Barrel_AlongX`. Air bombardment (`GEO_01AProj`): falls from Z 21244 and blows up on the arena floor.
- **Still broken / not tested:** Not tested on Windows or by playing. Only explosive, non-laser classes fly; others are logged "isn't flown yet" (52 projectile spawners on 15 maps, many with bolts, rockets or grenades). Missiles don't touch characters (`bActorDetonate`), don't bounce or stick; zone gravity other than the default, decals and per-surface effects aren't done. The trails' ribbon part isn't drawn (ribbon emitters). In one run a bombardment missile missed the level's collision and fell until its fuse ran out.
- **Next:** The landing gunship; clone troopers' rifles, skins and lighting; clones fighting droids.

## 2026-11-11: The menus' moving background video, played from the original file (rc_video, rc_game, rc_menus)

- **Changed:** New crate `rc_video` reads AVI files and decodes Microsoft MPEG-4 v2 (`MP42`) pictures, built on the MIT-licensed `oxideav-msmpeg4` with four fixes of ours (picture header, macroblock codes, slices, AC prediction). New tool `rc_video_check`. `rc_game` plays `GameData/Movies/menu_bg.avi` behind the original menus that name it (main menu, options, campaign, load), looping at its own 29.97 frames a second and darkened to 192/255 like the original's `BackgroundMovie` widget; not in the pause menu. `rc_menus` now reads the `BackgroundMovie` widget (`MovieWidget`) and has `render_over` and `quads::fit_box`. New script check "the main menu's background video plays"; the menu picture checks now paint over the same video frame.
- **Why:** User sent 9 side-by-side screenshots of the original and the port; the port's menus had a black background where the original shows this video.
- **Tested how:** `rc_video_check menu_bg.avi --compare` against ffmpeg's decode of all 475 frames; `cargo test -p rc_video -p rc_menus -p rc_game`; `rc_game --script` in the cloud with the game's menu files and video.
- **Result:** 95.86% of decoded numbers equal ffmpeg's, largest difference 4 of 255, average 0.04, no drift over the loop; about 3 to 4 ms a frame. Script: the original main menu, the video (20 frames shown in half a second, loops every 15.9 s) and the menu picture comparisons pass. The level checks failed in this run only because no maps were staged.
- **Still broken / not tested:** Not tested on Windows. In-mission menus don't draw the original's dark layer in place of the video yet. On non-4:3 screens the video's top and bottom (or sides) are cut off; that's our choice, the original only drew 4:3. Needs Rust 1.95 or newer.

## 2026-11-11: GRAPHICS options page as the original's script (rc_game, rc_menus, data/ui/original_menus.toml)

- **Changed:** GRAPHICS QUALITY now uses the five presets in the install's `System.ini` (read at start, `graphics_presets.rs`) and shows CUSTOM when the rows match none; SET RECOMMENDED uses the install's `DefaultConfig` preset and leaves BRIGHTNESS and VSYNC alone. BRIGHTNESS stores the original's `Gamma` (`video.gamma`, default 1.0, shown as 3). New rows: BUMPMAPPING QUALITY (`bump_quality`), PROJECTORS (`projectors`); BLOOM is NONE/LOW/HIGH (`bloom_quality`). PROJECTORS off greys out SQUAD SHADOWS; VSYNC off sets FSAA to NONE and greys it out. FSAA lists NONE plus what the graphics card supports (2X/4X/8X). Defaults are the HIGHEST preset (blur on, bloom HIGH, projectors and squad shadows on, bump LOW). The page opens with nothing lit. Settings `quality`, `brightness` and `bloom` are gone (old files log a warning for them and get the defaults).
- **Why:** User's side-by-side screenshots showed many differences (OFF instead of NONE, greyed rows, wrong defaults, SOUND lit). Strict rule: copy the original's files and behaviour.
- **Tested how:** The menu's script source and class defaults read from `XInterfaceCTMenus.u`; gamma formulas read from `Engine.dll` / `D3DDrv.dll` machine code in a scratch folder (nothing committed). `cargo test -p rc_game -p rc_menus`; `graphics_check` example under xvfb; `rc_game` clicked through in the cloud with the install's files.
- **Result:** All tests pass (new: preset reading, matching, CUSTOM skipping, row greying, FSAA list, gamma rows). `GRAPHICS CHECK: 34 passed, 0 failed`. In the cloud: page shows HIGHEST, 3, HIGHEST, HIGH, LOW, ON, HIGH, ON, ON, ON, NONE; greying and SET RECOMMENDED work.
- **Still broken / not tested:** Not tested on Windows. Brightness bends only the 3D view, matched at mid grey (the original bends the whole screen). Presets don't change the screen size (the user's choice, so wide screens keep their size). What bump quality and bloom LOW vs HIGH change is in the original's engine, so they look the same here. Character detail still shrinks character textures (the original drops mesh detail). No APPLY button or "keep these settings?" timer. The original's VSYNC arrows don't wrap (its style's `bNoWrap`), so from OFF press LEFT for ON.

## 2026-11-11: Menu letters and pictures at the original's size (rc_menus, rc_game, rc_probe)

- **Changed:** Menu pictures and letters are now sized for an 800x600 screen instead of 640x480 (`rc_menus::quads` BASE_WIDTH/BASE_HEIGHT), so everything is 0.8 times its old size. Stretched pictures' corners (frames, button bar ends) are drawn one texel to one screen pixel; the game tells the quad builder the menu's height in pixels (`quads::set_pixels_high`, set in `original_menu::draw`). `rc_probe --menu` can paint at another height with `RC_PROBE_MENU_HIGH` (e.g. 1200, to compare with a screenshot).
- **Why:** User's side-by-side screenshots: logo cut off at the left, OPTIONS title running into the box, frames too heavy, letters too big. The original's `XInterface.dll` sets ResScaleX = width x 1/800 and ResScaleY = height x 1/600 (both numbers are in the DLL), and its `DrawMenuSpriteStretched` makes each corner half the picture's texels at one texel per pixel. The old 640x480 was a guess from planets fitting their rings.
- **Tested how:** `rc_probe --menu` at 1200 pixels high next to the user's 1600x1200 shots of the original (options and main menu); `cargo test -p rc_menus`, `cargo test -p rc_game --bin rc_game original_menu`; `rc_game --script` in the cloud with the menu files only.
- **Result:** Title, tabs, rows, frame corners, bar ends, planets and logo line up with the original to a pixel or two. Script: every menu check passes (main menu matches its software picture: mean difference 3.3/255; campaign page 4.4/255); level checks fail only because no maps were staged.
- **Still broken / not tested:** Not tested on Windows. Pause and options pages not run through the GPU in the cloud this time (same drawing code). The original stretches menus sideways on wide screens; we keep the 4:3 size and spread them out, as before. Background video, pause menu rows, options values and PROFILES/EXTRAS are separate jobs.

## 2026-11-11: Pause menu shows and centres its rows as the original does (rc_menus, rc_game, rc_probe, data/ui/original_menus.toml)

- **Changed:** New `rc_menus::pause` and `rc_game/src/original_pause.rs`. Each time the pause menu opens it hides the rows the original hides and stacks the rest centred on the screen, as the original's pause menu script decides (read from `XInterfaceCTMenus.u` in the install; rewritten by hand). LOAD LAST SAVE shows only while down or dead; QUICK LOAD only with a quicksave; QUICK SAVE and SAVE GAME hide with no health, in a briefing or in the prologue; dead for good hides RESUME GAME and the dimming picture and darkens the text. ENABLE HINTS (`act:enable_hints`) shows only when the new `[game] hint_screens` setting is off (default on, as the original's `User.ini`) and the map's LevelInfo says it has hints; choosing it turns the setting on and resumes. Closing the load or save page hides LOAD LAST SAVE or QUICK LOAD when their saves are gone, without re-centring (as the original). New script check and `rc_probe --menu` pictures per case.
- **Why:** User's side-by-side screenshots: the port showed LOAD LAST SAVE and a greyed ENABLE HINTS while playing, and the rows weren't centred like the original's.
- **Tested how:** `cargo test -p rc_menus pause` (8 tests); `rc_probe --menu XInterfaceCTMenus.CTPausePCMenu` with the install's files; `rc_game --map geo_01a` in the cloud, pausing with the keyboard (xdotool).
- **Result:** In geo_01a the pause menu showed QUICK SAVE, RESUME GAME, RESTART LEVEL, SAVE GAME, LOAD GAME, OPTIONS, END MISSION, QUIT PROGRAM from 0.2742 down, focus on RESUME GAME. With `hint_screens = false`, ENABLE HINTS showed (geo_01a has hints); choosing it saved `hint_screens = true` and resumed, and the next pause hid it. Row positions in the probe's picture match the user's screenshot of the original within 3 pixels. The full `rc_game --script` was not run.
- **Still broken / not tested:** Not tested on Windows. Hint screens themselves (`ACTION_ShowDebriefingScreen` with `bIsForHintText`) aren't built. The row text is larger than the original's; that is the menu sizing work in another thread. The French/Spanish/Italian narrower rows aren't done (English only). Layering checked: the menu already draws over the HUD and subtitles; the subtitle text's look and place (bottom, our font) is not the original's and is a separate job.
- **Next:** Hint screens; the subtitle text's look.
## 2026-11-11: Effect spawners: gunships and fighters fly over the arena (rc_effects, rc_effects_plugin, rc_scripts_plugin, rc_game)

- **Changed:** The maps' `FXFactory` actors now run (`rc_effects::factory`, `rc_scripts_plugin/src/fx_factories.rs`), following the rules in ctgame.u's `Factory` script: off at the start, toggled by the event named by their tag, one effect at once then one every `SpawnInterval`..`SpawnIntervalMax` seconds, `MaxAlive`, `Capacity`/`bGenerateForever`, the four spawn modes, player range, `bCovert`, `SpawnEvent`/`ExhaustedEvent`, `ACTION_ResetFactory`; `EmitterDrawScale` and `SpawnOffset` from `FXFactory`. On/off and what is left are saved (`effect_spawners`). Mesh emitters now read `CoordinateSystem` (relative ones turn with the effect), `UseMeshBlendMode` (models drawn solid) and the effect's `bUnlit` (lit models take the level's light at their place). `SpawnEffect` gains `rotation`, `draw_scale` and `id`; new `EffectEnded` message. New settings `lit_model_light`, `lit_model_scale` in `data/effects/effects.toml`.
- **Why:** User's side-by-side screenshots of geo_01a: the original has gunships and fighters overhead and a battle going on; ours had none. They come from five `OverheadVehicles` spawners (`cFX_Gunships`, `cFX_GEOfighters`) switched on by `ScriptedTrigger52` after the briefing, and `BattleExplosions` near the player.
- **Tested how:** `cargo test -p rc_effects -p rc_effects_plugin -p rc_scripts_plugin` (5 new spawner tests, 1 relative-turn test, 1 map-reading test); `rc_game --script` in the cloud with the game's files: new waits "the opening script switches on the overhead flyovers" and "gunships and fighters fly over, high above the arena", picture `flyover`.
- **Result:** 133 checks passed, 0 failed. The picture shows Geonosian fighters crossing the sky.
- **Still broken / not tested:** Not tested on Windows or by playing. Our reading, not checked against the engine code: draw scale multiplies particle size; the relative turn is the effect's full rotation; the flyovers' small Z spin isn't applied; lit models start from the droids' light level (0.5); fades aren't applied to models; `bCovert` uses the camera's 4:3 field of view. Still missing from the user's screenshots: projectile spawners (air bombardment, bunker missiles), the landing gunship's animations, clone troopers' rifles, skins and lighting, and clones fighting droids.
- **Next:** Projectile spawners (`ProjectileFactory`).

## 2026-11-09: The squad keeps their health and order on the next map (rc_squad_plugin, rc_save)

- **Changed:** New `crates/rc_squad_plugin/src/carry.rs`: a `[squad_carry]` save section with each squad mate's health and the squad's order, carried to the next map and put back once the squad appears there. `data/levels/travel.toml` (and `rc_save`'s default) now carries `["combat", "squad_carry"]`. `rc_game_core::save::CarryingOver` exists while `rc_save` writes the carried sections, so the squad's section stays out of ordinary save files. New test `crates/rc_squad_plugin/tests/carry_over.rs`.

Read from the game's own CTCharacters.u (`SaveSquadTravelInfo`, `TravelPostAccept`, not copied): each mate's health is kept; a mate who is down gets a quarter of full health; the last order is kept unless it was "secure position".

- **Still broken / not tested:** Not tested on Windows, by playing, or with `rc_game --script` (no game files staged here). The original's health-level look (`SetHealthLevel`) isn't shown. A mate carried but not on the next map is dropped; the original keeps them for a later map.
- **Next:** The user plays from geo_01a into geo_01b with a hurt squad.
